# Spec revision 61: what changed in the records

Generated by `tools/record_changes.py`; do not edit. Each generated record as committed at
`66ba6f3` (2026-11-14: the records of revision 59's regeneration and its stage-end sweep, which
revision 60 left as they were), against the record after revision 61's one regeneration, read at commit `d7fba85`, the last before revision 62's regeneration moved the records; both sides are read from git, so this page is frozen.
Revision 61 (T-243 and the rest of T-004) changed rules: a self-directed rise converts borrowed pseudo-self
(`M1.A.5c`), the loaded-tie gate and the demand share read perspective on that tie (`M1.A.18d`), and every
belief write is its own log record (`M16.A.5`). A changed verdict is therefore a **new result under new
rules**, reported beside revision 59's and version 1's verdicts and never in their place (`M11.F`); a premise
passing is never a finding (`M11.5`). It is not a
defect. A row's verdict is its outcome (a criterion, or a criterion under a sweep setting) or whether the
criterion caught the mutant (a mutation row). An effect **moved** when its mean difference changed by more than
the half-width revision 59's record stated; moved effects are listed only for rows whose verdict held. "Differ at
all" counts every field of a row on both sides that is not exactly revision 59's (the verdict, the seed count, the
flags, each readout's mean difference, half-width and p), including a field on one side only; each is listed
under its record, and "No change" means none differs. A record's hash line must differ from revision 59's, or the
tool refuses: an unchanged hash means it was not regenerated. A record with no row on both sides is refused too.

- `docs/review/phase_c_trace_seed7.md, docs/review/phase_c_chart_seed7.html`: one seed's run, rendered; S1.7a adds lock-in release rows by design, so `git diff 66ba6f3` on them shows that and any change of trajectory.
- `docs/review/phase_b_trace_seed7.md, docs/review/phase_b_trace_seed7_nadia.md`: Phase B's seed-7 traces, rendered at Phase B's close (`3132c69`) and not regenerated since, so they are revision 59's.
- `docs/phase_c_completion_report.md §11`: generated from the Phase C records compared here (`tools/c16_report.py`).
- `docs/spec_coverage.md`: generated from the tests and the ensemble and mutation records compared here.
- `docs/STATUS.md`: generated by `tools/status.py` from the spec, its index, the revision log and the records compared here (T-225, 2026-11-11); it holds no result of its own.
- `docs/phase_b_mutation_record.md`: the record of Phase B's close at commit `3132c69`, frozen (plan D5) and not regenerated, so it is revision 59's apart from the status line stage 1 added; `tools/mutation_gate.py` refuses to rewrite it.
